# Odoo

<img src="../../.gitbook/assets/connectors/odoo.png" alt="Odoo logo" width="64">

Odoo is an open-source suite of business apps (CRM, Sales, Invoicing, Inventory, Purchase, Project, Employees, Calendar and more). This connector uses Odoo's JSON-2 external API (POST /json/2/&lt;model&gt;/&lt;method&gt;, Odoo 19+ and Odoo Online) with a user API key, so it can search, read, create, update and delete records in any Odoo model the API key's user can access.

| | |
| --- | --- |
| **Category** | [Other](README.md) |
| **Website** | [odoo.com](https://odoo.com) |
| **Authentication** | Credentials you enter |
| **Slug** | `odoo` |

## Use it

Add Odoo from **Build → Integrations → Connectors** and connect an account, as in [Connect a system](../../build/connectors/connect-a-system.md). In workflow code, call an action as `fastn.connector.odoo.<action>()`, with the action's slug from the table below. See [Workflow runtime API](../../reference/workflow-runtime.md).

## Actions

| Action | Slug | What it does |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Cancel Invoice | `cancelInvoice` | Cancels a draft invoice. Runs button_cancel on Odoo invoices (account.move). Returns what Odoo returns (often true, null or an action description). |
| Cancel Payment | `cancelPayment` | Cancels a payment. Runs action_cancel on Odoo payments (account.payment). Returns what Odoo returns (often true, null or an action description). |
| Cancel Purchase Order | `cancelPurchaseOrder` | Cancels a purchase order. Runs button_cancel on Odoo purchase orders (purchase.order). Returns what Odoo returns (often true, null or an action description). |
| Cancel Sales Order | `cancelSalesOrder` | Cancels a quotation or sales order. Runs action_cancel on Odoo sales orders (sale.order). Returns what Odoo returns (often true, null or an action description). |
| Cancel Transfer | `cancelTransfer` | Cancels a transfer. Runs action_cancel on Odoo transfers (stock.picking). Returns what Odoo returns (often true, null or an action description). |
| Check Transfer Availability | `checkTransferAvailability` | Reserves available stock for a confirmed transfer. Runs action_assign on Odoo transfers (stock.picking). Returns what Odoo returns (often true, null or an action description). |
| Confirm Payment | `confirmPayment` | Confirms (posts) a draft payment. Runs action_post on Odoo payments (account.payment). Returns what Odoo returns (often true, null or an action description). |
| Confirm Purchase Order | `confirmPurchaseOrder` | Confirms a request for quotation into a purchase order. Runs button_confirm on Odoo purchase orders (purchase.order). Returns what Odoo returns (often true, null or an action description). |
| Confirm Sales Order | `confirmSalesOrder` | Confirms a quotation and turns it into a sales order. Runs action_confirm on Odoo sales orders (sale.order). Returns what Odoo returns (often true, null or an action description). |
| Count Records | `countRecords` | Counts the records of any Odoo model that match a domain filter (ORM search_count). Returns the number of matching records. |

| Mark Transfer As To Do | `markTransferAsToDo` | Confirms a draft transfer so stock can be reserved. Runs action_confirm on Odoo transfers (stock.picking). Returns what Odoo returns (often true, null or an action description). |
| Post Invoice | `postInvoice` | Validates (posts) a draft invoice or bill. Runs action_post on Odoo invoices (account.move). Returns what Odoo returns (often true, null or an action description). |
| Read Records | `readRecords` | Reads one or more records of any Odoo model by ID and returns the requested fields (ORM read). Returns an array of record objects. |
| Reset Invoice To Draft | `resetInvoiceToDraft` | Sets a posted or cancelled invoice back to draft. Runs button_draft on Odoo invoices (account.move). Returns what Odoo returns (often true, null or an action description). |
| Reset Payment To Draft | `resetPaymentToDraft` | Sets a payment back to draft. Runs action_draft on Odoo payments (account.payment). Returns what Odoo returns (often true, null or an action description). |
| Reset Purchase Order To Draft | `resetPurchaseOrderToDraft` | Sets a cancelled purchase order back to draft. Runs button_draft on Odoo purchase orders (purchase.order). Returns what Odoo returns (often true, null or an action description). |
| Reset Sales Order To Quotation | `resetSalesOrderToQuotation` | Sets a cancelled sales order back to a draft quotation. Runs action_draft on Odoo sales orders (sale.order). Returns what Odoo returns (often true, null or an action description). |
| Run Record Action | `runRecordAction` | Runs a button-style business action on Odoo records that takes no extra arguments, for example action_confirm on sale.order (confirm a quotation), action_post on account.move (post an invoice), button_confirm on purchase.order or action_set_won on crm.lead. Returns the value the Odoo method returns (true, null or an action description). |
| Search Read Records | `searchReadRecords` | Finds records of any Odoo model that match a domain filter and returns the requested fields in one call (ORM search_read). Use it to list contacts (res.partner), leads (crm.lead), sales orders (sale.order), invoices (account.move), products (product.product) and so on. Returns an array of record objects; each has id plus the requested fields. |
| Search Records | `searchRecords` | Finds records of any Odoo model that match a domain filter and returns only their IDs (ORM search). Use Read Records afterwards to load fields. Returns an array of record IDs. |
| Search Records By Name | `searchRecordsByName` | Searches any Odoo model by display name and returns matching ID and name pairs (ORM name_search), the same lookup Odoo uses for dropdowns. Useful to find a customer, product or user ID from a name. Returns an array of [id, display name] pairs. |

Only the fields you pass are changed. Odoo model ir.attachment (A Mixin That Contains The Generic Methods For All Models That Need To Be Embedded). Returns true on success. |
